{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeNFDHm1Qc7Hp3WcfGM5L","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Q2x7wy88sQFkQtuEKAhHY3","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > parts/cable_clip.md <<'MD'\n# cable_clip\n\n## What it is\n\nA screw-down clip that traps one cable bundle against a surface. An open-top\nchannel runs the full length of the part along Y, the cable drops in from above,\nand a flat tab off one side takes a single screw into the wall or bench.\n\nThe bundle is measured: `bundle_diameter` is 8.0mm on file in\n`measurements.toml`, calipered across the taped bundle. Everything else is\nderived from it, so the same file prints a clip for a fatter or thinner bundle\nby moving one number.\n\n## Design notes\n\n- **Channel 8.4 wide by 8.0 deep.** Width is the bundle plus 0.4 of clearance,\n  the fits table's snug-to-moving band, so the bundle drops in without being\n  forced and without rattling. Depth is the bundle diameter exactly: the clip\n  captures the bundle to its full width rather than pinching it, and a screw\n  through the tab holds the assembly down.\n- **Square section, on purpose.** The floor is one flat face the full 8.4mm of\n  channel width and both inner walls are unbroken 8.0 x 12.0 rectangles. No\n  chamfer of any size is allowed to reach them, which is why the polish filter\n  drops every edge whose span touches the channel rather than only the edges\n  lying in it. A rounded channel corner would let a round bundle ride up out of\n  the square section it is supposed to sit in.\n- **Walls 2.4, base 3.0.** 2.4 is comfortably over the 2mm minimum and prints as\n  three clean beads either side. The base is 3.0 because it is also the tab, and\n  the tab is what the screw bears on.\n- **Tab 10.0 long, hole 4.2.** 4.2 is an M4 shaft with room to shift the clip a\n  little while the screw is loose; it is not the ISO clearance column, which\n  would be 4.5, because this hole locates the clip rather than just passing a\n  screw. Centred in the tab it leaves 2.9mm of tab beyond the bore and 3.9mm to\n  each side.\n- **Prints as it sits.** Flat bottom on the bed, 278mm2 of first layer, the\n  channel opening straight up and the screw bore vertical. Nothing overhangs,\n  nothing bridges, no supports.\n- **Chamfers are 1.2mm, not the usual 1.0.** Three of them meet at each free\n  convex corner of the tab, and the corner triangle they leave is `0.866 * size\n  ** 2`: at 1.0mm that is 0.87mm2, a sliver, and at 1.2mm it is 1.25mm2, a face.\n  Sizing the chamfer so the part earns no slivers at all beats declaring six of\n  them, and 1.2 still reads as one consistent facet size on a part this small.\n\n## Don't\n\n- **Don't chamfer the channel mouth, the channel floor edges, or the top rim\n  corners at the Y ends.** All three are lead-ins onto fit geometry and the\n  requirement is a square channel. The earlier version chamfered the wall top\n  edges at the Y ends, which nicked 1.44mm2 out of the top corner of each inner\n  wall and made the channel walls something other than plain rectangles.\n- **Don't chamfer the screw bore.** A 1.2mm lead-in on a 3.0mm tab eats 40% of\n  the bearing depth under the screw head, and it fired `min_wall` at 0.41mm\n  where the cone met the tab's own top chamfers.\n- **Don't counterbore the tab.** The tab is 3.0 thick and a cap head pocket\n  would leave about 0.5mm under the screw. Use a washer if the head digs in.\n- **Don't put a lid, a snap arm or a hinged flap over the channel.** This clip is\n  screwed down over the bundle; a snap arm at this scale would be a 2mm web\n  bending across layer welds, which the flexure rules veto outright.\n- **Don't round the rim.** Chamfer is the default everywhere, and `crown` was\n  not used because this wall is not a closed perimeter.\n\n## Changelog\n\n- 2026-08-24: First version. 8.0mm bundle, 23.2 x 12.0 x 11.0, clean checks at\n  6, 8 and 12mm bundles.\n\n```toml\n[part]\nmin_wall = 2.4\n\n[variants.cable_clip_6mm]\nnote = \"The same clip for a thinner bundle: 6mm across instead of 8.\"\n\n[variants.cable_clip_6mm.params]\nbundle_diameter = 6.0\n\n[variants.cable_clip_12mm]\nnote = \"The same clip for a fat bundle, 12mm across, for a mains lead or a loom.\"\n\n[variants.cable_clip_12mm.params]\nbundle_diameter = 12.0\n```\nMD\nnurb check cable_clip 2>&1|tail -10","description":"Write card with variants and check all"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":583,"cache_read_input_tokens":70998,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":583},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8918e3b3-761e-422c-ab89-7ce2313cd8ff","uuid":"575b0cfd-4daf-44e0-90d4-450619ec3821","timestamp":"2026-08-24T21:00:23.262Z","request_id":"req_011CeNFDH2rSZMxngAR6aNWw"}
